- **v-orchestrator-auditor**: Read-only auditor of the local /v orchestrator system (skills, hooks, agents, validators, tests under ~/.claude). Dispatched by /v-self-audit to run one audit / efficiency / testing-audit / synthesis stage and return a bounded artifact. Verifies every claim against disk; never edits source. ([heat23/agentic-software-delivery](https://github.com/heat23/agentic-software-delivery/blob/HEAD/agents/v-orchestrator-auditor.md))
- **v-pre-flight-runner**: Runs /v pre-flight quality gates (TSC, PHPStan, lint, build, pest, vitest, audits) and writes PRE_FLIGHT_REPORT_&lt;sid&gt;.md. READ-ONLY: must not Edit/Write source files. If gates fail, capture failures in the report and return — do NOT 'fix' issues found. ([heat23/agentic-software-delivery](https://github.com/heat23/agentic-software-delivery/blob/HEAD/agents/v-pre-flight-runner.md))
- **v-qa-reviewer**: Independent QA acceptance reviewer for /v Step 6.4.9. Judges the PRODUCT, not the code: acceptance against the user's ORIGINAL request, exploratory/adversarial testing beyond the scripted scenarios, cross-feature regression, and test quality. Writes QA_REPORT_&lt;sid&gt;.md with domain+severity-tagged findings and a pass/fail verdict. READ-ONLY on source — it assesses, it never fixes (the orchestrator's SME-driven remediation loop owns fixes). ([heat23/agentic-software-delivery](https://github.com/heat23/agentic-software-delivery/blob/HEAD/agents/v-qa-reviewer.md))
- **V-range**: FINDING | golden fixture output of V-range location: circuit/transfer.go:40 ([shramee/zk-audit](https://github.com/shramee/zk-audit/blob/HEAD/testdata/assemble/partial/agents/V-range.md))
- **v-ux-critique-reviewer**: Runs UX heuristic walkthrough against changed UI files in /v Step 3.5 and writes UX_CRITIQUE_&lt;sid&gt;.md. READ-ONLY: covers contrast, focus states, microcopy, state coverage, hierarchy, scannability, Fitts' law, cognitive load, affordance, consistency. Findings are advisory. ([heat23/agentic-software-delivery](https://github.com/heat23/agentic-software-delivery/blob/HEAD/agents/v-ux-critique-reviewer.md))
- **v-verify-done-runner**: Runs /v verify-done convention checks against the session's diff and RETURNS the VERIFY_DONE_REPORT_&lt;sid&gt;.md content for the orchestrator to persist. READ-ONLY: cannot Edit/Write (enforced via disallowedTools). Convention violations are reported, not auto-fixed. ([heat23/agentic-software-delivery](https://github.com/heat23/agentic-software-delivery/blob/HEAD/agents/v-verify-done-runner.md))
- **v-workflow-verifier**: Drives changed user-facing workflows during /v Step 3.5 and writes WORKFLOW_VERIFICATION_&lt;sid&gt;.md. Builds the front-end, boots the app, and generates+commits a deterministic Playwright spec (run via the `npx playwright test` CLI) covering the golden path and sad paths (empty/loading/error/slow/permission/concurrent/double-submit) — asserting zero console errors and zero failed requests. Degrades loudly (never silent, never hard-blocks) when the browser env is unavailable. ([heat23/agentic-software-delivery](https://github.com/heat23/agentic-software-delivery/blob/HEAD/agents/v-workflow-verifier.md))
